{"output": "def _build_power_capabilities(self) -> None:\n        \"\"\"pdu.* reads + power.* reads via Server Manager (plan §27).\n\n        NO power mutation exists for ANY role here: outlet power_off is\n        DESTRUCTIVE (deny-for-all) and pdu.request_reboot rides the durable\n        approval path (SENSITIVE_WRITE) with SM-side actuation still env-gated.\n        \"\"\"\n        if self.power is None:\n            return  # power domain absent when SM unconfigured/disabled\n\n        def _require_power():\n            client = self.power\n            if client is None:\n                raise DomainUnavailableError(\n                    \"power domain not configured (SM base/token missing or disabled)\")\n            return client\n\n        # ---- resources (READ) ----\n\n        def r_pdu_list(identity, uri_params):\n            return _require_power().pdu_list()\n\n        def r_pdu_status(identity, uri_params):\n            caps = _require_power().pdu_capabilities()\n            return {\"capabilities\": caps,\n                    \"note\": \"backend is the PDU Manager contract (real backend)\"}\n\n        def r_pdu_assets(identity, uri_params):\n            return _require_power().pdu_assets()\n\n        def r_pdu_outlet_status(identity, uri_params):\n            key = (uri_params or {}).get(\"pdu\") or \"\"\n            outlet = (uri_params or {}).get(\"outlet\") or \"\"\n            if not key or not str(outlet).isdigit():\n                raise ValidationError_(\"pdu key and numeric outlet are required\")\n            return _require_power().pdu_outlet_status(key, int(outlet))\n\n        def _totals(body):\n            \"\"\"W5 live-found: SM nests the aggregate under ``totals`` (a dict with\n            label/definition/kwh_24h/cost_usd_24h/kwh_30d/cost_usd_30d/\n            incomplete_reason). Reading body[\"total\"]/body[\"incomplete_reason\"]\n            always produced null even with all channels fresh — TOTAL\n            MARION_IA_USA was silently blank. Withhold logic: when ANY channel\n            is stale the total is null WITH the explicit incomplete_reason\n            (never zeroed, never fabricated).\"\"\"\n            totals = body.get(\"totals\") or {}\n            channels = body.get(\"channels\") or []\n            any_stale = bool(channels) and any(c.get(\"stale\") for c in channels)\n            if any_stale:\n                return {\"total\": None,\n                        \"incomplete_reason\": (totals.get(\"incomplete_reason\")\n                                              or \"one or more channels stale; \"\n                                              \"TOTAL withheld (never zeroed)\")}\n            if not channels:\n                return {\"total\": None,\n                        \"incomplete_reason\": \"no channel data returned by Server Manager\"}\n            return {\"total\": totals or None,\n                    \"incomplete_reason\": totals.get(\"incomplete_reason\")}\n\n        def r_power_current(identity, uri_params):", "exit_code": 0, "error": null}

{"content": "1|\"\"\"ACMS adapter (plan §18): the FIRST domain adapter.\n2|\n3|The gateway calls ACMS REST (the domain authority) over HTTPS with the\n4|gateway's own bearer credential (ACMS_ADMIN_TOKEN scope; later a dedicated\n5|service token). No DB session is opened here in production paths — REST is\n6|the boundary, keeping the gateway independently fail-able (plan §33: gateway\n7|failure never breaks ACMS, and ACMS failure never breaks REST/A2A/SSE).\n8|\n9|Surface (plan §18):\n10|\n11|Resources (READ):\n12|  acms.work.get            (URI: acms://work/{work_uid_or_id})\n13|  acms.work.current        (URI: acms://assignment/current)\n14|  acms.project.get         (URI: acms://project/{project_id_or_slug})\n15|  acms.product.get         (URI: acms://product/{product_id})\n16|  acms.artifact.get        (URI: acms://artifact/{artifact_uid_or_id})\n17|  acms.agent.self          (URI: acms://agent/self)\n18|  acms.policy.current      (URI: acms://policy/current)\n19|  acms.inbox.current       (URI: acms://inbox/current)\n20|  acms.context.current     (URI: acms://context/current)  <- plan §14 manifest\n21|\n22|Worker tools (SAFE_WRITE unless noted):\n23|  acms.work.update_progress     (scope: own assignment)\n24|  acms.work.mark_blocked        (scope: own assignment)\n25|  acms.artifact.create          (scope: own assignment)\n26|  acms.work.submit_handoff      (scope: own assignment)\n27|  acms.inbox.raise              (scope: own assignment; severity-bounded)\n28|  acms.review.request           (scope: own assignment)\n29|\n30|Scope rules:\n31|- agent token (worker role): may read Work/Project/Product/Artifacts linked to\n32|  its CURRENT ACTIVE assignment + general non-sensitive metadata; may write\n33|  only within its own assignment (work/assignment/artifact linkage enforced).\n34|- assignment token: narrowed to exactly its Work UID (+ project/jira binding).\n35|- executive/infrastructure_admin: broader reads; still no DESTRUCTIVE ops in W1.\n36|\"\"\"\n37|from __future__ import annotations\n38|\n39|import base64\n40|import json\n41|import ssl\n42|import urllib.error\n43|import urllib.request\n44|from typing import Any\n45|\n46|from .errors import DomainUnavailableError, NotFoundError, OutOfScopeError, ValidationError_\n47|from .models import CallIdentity, RiskClass, Role\n48|from .policy import Capability\n49|\n50|\n51|class AcmsClient:\n52|    \"\"\"Thin REST client for ACMS. TLS verification ON by default; CT122 uses a\n53|    self-signed cert, so the gateway trusts the local CA file via env\n54|    (ACMS_TLS_CA) or (explicitly, config-gated) disables verification for the\n55|    internal raw-IP endpoint.\"\"\"\n56|\n57|    def __init__(self, base_url: str, token: str, *, tls_ca: str | None = None,\n58|                 insecure_tls: bool = False, timeout_seconds: int = 15):\n59|        if not base_url:\n60|            raise ValueError(\"ACMS base URL not configured\")\n61|", "total_lines": 208, "file_size": 9488, "truncated": true, "hint": "Use offset=61 to continue reading (showing 1-60 of 208 lines)", "is_binary": false, "is_image": false}

{"content": "61|        if not token:\n62|            raise ValueError(\"ACMS service token not configured\")\n63|        self.base_url = base_url.rstrip(\"/\")\n64|        self._token = token\n65|        self.timeout = timeout_seconds\n66|        if insecure_tls:\n67|            ctx = ssl.create_default_context()\n68|            ctx.check_hostname = False\n69|            ctx.verify_mode = ssl.CERT_NONE\n70|            self._ctx: ssl.SSLContext | None = ctx\n71|        elif tls_ca:\n72|            self._ctx = ssl.create_default_context(cafile=tls_ca)\n73|        else:\n74|            self._ctx = None  # default verification\n75|\n76|    def request(self, method: str, path: str, payload: dict[str, Any] | None = None,\n77|                query: dict[str, str] | None = None) -> tuple[int, Any]:\n78|        url = f\"{self.base_url}{path}\"\n79|        if query:\n80|            from urllib.parse import urlencode\n81|            url += \"?\" + urlencode(query)\n82|        data = None\n83|        headers = {\"Authorization\": f\"Bearer {self._token}\", \"Accept\": \"application/json\"}\n84|        if payload is not None:\n85|            data = json.dumps(payload).encode(\"utf-8\")\n86|            headers[\"Content-Type\"] = \"application/json\"\n87|        req = urllib.request.Request(url, data=data, headers=headers, method=method)\n88|        try:\n89|            with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=self.timeout, context=self._ctx) as resp:\n90|                body = resp.read().decode(\"utf-8\", errors=\"replace\")\n91|                status = resp.status\n92|        except urllib.error.HTTPError as exc:\n93|            body = exc.read().decode(\"utf-8\", errors=\"replace\")\n94|            status = exc.code\n95|        except (urllib.error.URLError, TimeoutError, OSError) as exc:\n96|            raise DomainUnavailableError(f\"ACMS unreachable: {exc.__class__.__name__}\") from exc\n97|        try:\n98|            parsed = json.loads(body) if body else None\n99|        except ValueError:\n100|            parsed = body\n101|        return status, parsed\n102|\n103|    # ---------------- lookups ----------------\n104|\n105|    def find_work_item(self, ref: str) -> dict[str, Any] | None:\n106|        \"\"\"Resolve a Work reference: UID, short key, or UUID. None when absent.\"\"\"\n107|        if not ref:\n108|            return None\n109|        status, body = self.request(\"GET\", f\"/api/v1/work/items/{ref}\")\n110|        if status == 200:\n111|            return body\n112|        # list + filter by UID/key (the detail route may accept only UUIDs)\n113|        status, body = self.request(\"GET\", \"/api/v1/work/items\")\n114|        if status != 200 or not isinstance(body, list):\n115|            raise DomainUnavailableError(f\"ACMS work list failed (HTTP {status})\")\n116|        for item in body:\n117|            if ref in (item.get(\"work_item_id\"), item.get(\"work_key\"), item.get(\"work_uid\")):\n118|                return item\n119|        return None\n120|\n121|    def active_assignment(self, agent_id: str) -> dict[str, Any] | None:\n122|        status, body = self.request(\"GET\", \"/api/v1/work/assignments\", query={\"agent_id\": agent_id, \"status\": \"ACTIVE\"})\n123|        if status != 200 or not isinstance(body, list):\n124|            raise DomainUnavailableError(f\"ACMS assignments failed (HTTP {status})\")\n125|        return body[0] if body else None\n126|\n127|    def get_project(self, ref: str) -> dict[str, Any] | None:\n128|        status, body = self.request(\"GET\", f\"/projects/{ref}\")\n129|        if status == 200:\n130|            return body\n131|        status, body = self.request(\"GET\", \"/projects\")\n132|        if status != 200 or not isinstance(body, list):\n133|            raise DomainUnavailableError(f\"ACMS projects failed (HTTP {status})\")\n134|        for p in body:\n135|            if ref in (p.get(\"project_id\"), p.get(\"slug\"), p.get(\"name\")):\n136|                return p\n137|        return None\n138|\n139|    def get_product(self, product_id: str) -> dict[str, Any] | None:\n140|        status, body = self.request(\"GET\", f\"/products/{product_id}\")\n141|        if status == 200:\n142|            return body\n143|        return None\n144|\n145|    def find_artifact(self, ref: str, *, work_item_id: str | None = None) -> dict[str, Any] | None:\n146|        status, body = self.request(\"GET\", f\"/artifacts/{ref}\")\n147|        if status == 200:\n148|            return body\n149|        # artifact detail route accepts UUID/UID/sha; fall back to list filter\n150|        status, body = self.request(\"GET\", \"/artifacts\")\n151|        if status != 200 or not isinstance(body, list):\n152|            raise DomainUnavailableError(f\"ACMS artifacts failed (HTTP {status})\")\n153|        for a in body:\n154|            if ref in (a.get(\"artifact_id\"), a.get(\"artifact_uid\")):\n155|                return a\n156|        return None\n157|\n158|    def artifact_content(self, artifact_id: str) -> str | None:\n159|        status, body = self.request(\"GET\", f\"/artifacts/{artifact_id}/content\")\n160|        if status != 200:\n161|            return None\n162|        if isinstance(body, dict):\n163|            return body.get(\"content\")\n164|        return body if isinstance(body, str) else None\n165|\n166|    def get_agent(self, agent_id: str) -> dict[str, Any] | None:\n167|        status, body = self.request(\"GET\", f\"/api/v1/agents/{agent_id}\")\n168|        if status == 200:\n169|            return body\n170|        status, body = self.request(\"GET\", \"/api/v1/agents\")\n171|        if status != 200 or not isinstance(body, list):\n172|            raise DomainUnavailableError(f\"ACMS agents failed (HTTP {status})\")\n173|        for a in body:\n174|            if ref_match(agent_id, a):\n175|                return a\n176|        return None\n177|\n178|    # ---------------- scope enforcement helper ----------------\n179|\n180|    @staticmethod\n181|    def assert_assignment_scope(identity: CallIdentity, work: dict[str, Any],\n182|                                assignment: dict[str, Any] | None) -> None:\n183|        \"\"\"Raise OutOfScopeError unless identity may act on this work item.\"\"\"\n184|        if identity.has_role(\"executive\", \"infrastructure_admin\"):\n185|            return\n186|        if identity.is_assignment_scoped:\n187|            if identity.work_uid and work.get(\"work_uid\") != identity.work_uid                and work.get(\"work_key\") != identity.work_uid                and work.get(\"work_item_id\") != identity.work_uid:\n188|                raise OutOfScopeError(\n189|                    f\"assignment token is bound to {identity.work_uid}; \"\n190|                    f\"{work.get('work_uid') or work.get('work_item_id')} is out of scope\"\n191|                )\n192|            return\n193|        # agent token: needs the work item to be its own ACTIVE assignment\n194|        if assignment is None or assignment.get(\"agent_id\") not in (identity.acms_agent_id,):\n195|            raise OutOfScopeError(\n196|                \"SAFE_WRITE requires an ACTIVE assignment for your agent \"\n197|                \"(or an assignment-scoped token)\"\n198|            )\n199|\n200|\n201|def ref_match(agent_ref: str, agent_obj: dict[str, Any]) -> bool:\n202|    return agent_ref in (\n203|        agent_obj.get(\"agent_id\"),\n204|        agent_obj.get(\"display_name\"),\n205|        agent_obj.get(\"external_registration_id\"),\n206|        agent_obj.get(\"legacy_name\"),\n207|        agent_obj.get(\"worker_uid\") and f\"uid-{agent_obj.get('worker_uid')}\",\n208|    )\n209|", "total_lines": 208, "file_size": 9488, "truncated": false, "is_binary": false, "is_image": false}

{"output": "class GatewayServer:\n    \"\"\"Owns the FastMCP instance + registry + adapters; exposes the ASGI app.\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, config: GatewayConfig, *, tokens: TokenStore, audit: AuditLog,\n                 acms_client: AcmsClient | None = None,\n                 llm_client: ServerManagerClient | None = None,\n                 github_client: GithubClient | None = None,\n                 sandbox_client: \"SandboxClient | None\" = None,\n                 power_client: ServerManagerClient | None = None,\n                 jira_client: \"JiraMcpClient | None\" = None,\n                 registry_client: \"RegistryClient | None\" = None,\n                 monitoring_client: \"MonitoringClient | None\" = None,\n                 approvals: ApprovalStore | None = None):\n        self.config = config\n        self.tokens = tokens\n        self.audit = audit\n        self.approvals = approvals or ApprovalStore(config.approvals_path)\n        self.registry = PolicyRegistry()\n        self.acms = acms_client or AcmsClient(\n            config.acms_base_url,\n            config.acms_token,\n            tls_ca=config.acms_tls_ca,\n            insecure_tls=config.acms_insecure_tls,\n        )\n        self.llm: ServerManagerClient | None = llm_client or (\n            ServerManagerClient(config.llm_base_url, config.llm_token)\n            if config.llm_base_url and config.llm_token else None\n        )\n        self.github: GithubClient | None = github_client or (\n            GithubClient(config.github_token,\n                         repos_allowlist=_parse_list(config.github_repos))\n            if config.github_token else None\n        )\n        # W4 (plan §26): sandbox domain rides the SM machine API — same\n        # endpoint as llm.*, dedicated SandboxClient (ARM surface) so the\n        # domains can be gated apart.\n        self.sandbox: SandboxClient | None = sandbox_client or (\n            SandboxClient(config.llm_base_url, config.llm_token)\n            if (config.proxmox_enabled and config.llm_base_url and config.llm_token)\n            else None\n        )\n        # W5 (plan §27): pdu.*/power.* ride the SAME SM machine API; dedicated\n        # PowerClient so the domain is gated apart (same transport, new surface).\n        self.power: ServerManagerClient | None = power_client or (\n            # W5 live-found: the per-asset power-state read fans out to the PDU\n            # Manager's SSH state poll (~20s cold). The client default (10s)\n            # times out before SM answers; raise the power client to 45s.\n            PowerClient(config.llm_base_url, config.llm_token, timeout_seconds=45)\n            if (config.llm_base_url and config.llm_token) else None\n        )\n        # W6 (plan §28): jira.* rides the gateway's own Jira REST credential.\n        self.jira = jira_client or (\n            JiraMcpClient(config.jira_base_url, config.jira_email,\n                          config.jira_api_token,\n                          allowed_projects=config.jira_projects)\n            if (config.jira_base_url and config.jira_email and config.jira_api_token)\n            else None\n        )\n        self.jira_mutation_enabled = bool(config.jira_status_mutation_enabled)\n        # W7 (plan §29): registry.* + monitoring.* read-only context surfaces.\n        self.svc_registry = registry_client or (\n            RegistryClient(config.registry_base_url, config.registry_token)\n            if (config.registry_base_url and config.registry_token) else None\n        )\n        self.kuma = monitoring_client or (\n            MonitoringClient(config.kuma_url, config.kuma_user, config.kuma_pass)\n            if (config.kuma_url and config.kuma_user and config.kuma_pass) else None\n        )\n        self.internal = InternalApi(tokens=tokens, audit=audit, approvals=self.approvals,\n                                    internal_token=config.internal_token)\n        self.executive_names = set(_parse_list(config.executive_agent_names))\n\n        transport_security = TransportSecuritySettings(\n            enable_dns_rebinding_protection=bool(config.allowed_hosts),\n            allowed_hosts=_parse_list(config.allowed_hosts),\n        ) if config.allowed_hosts else TransportSecuritySettings(\n            enable_dns_rebinding_protection=False\n        )\n\n        self.mcp = FastMCP(\n            \"miam-mcp-gateway\",\n            instructions=(\n                \"MIAM internal MCP gateway. Internal Startup Teams agents only. \"\n                \"Auth via Bearer agent/assignment token on every request. \"\n                \"Domains: acms.* in W1; llm/runtime/github/proxmox/pdu/power/jira/registry later.\"\n            ),\n            stateless_http=True,\n            json_response=True,\n            transport_security=transport_security,\n            host=config.host,\n            port=config.port,\n        )\n\n        self._build_acms_capabilities()\n        self._build_llm_runtime_capabilities()\n        self._build_github_capabilities()\n        self._build_proxmox_capabilities()\n        self._build_power_capabilities()\n        self._build_jira_capabilities()", "exit_code": 0, "error": null}

Empty disables protection\n    # (default SDK behavior); prod config SHOULD list the gateway host.\n    allowed_hosts: str = \"\"\n    # ---- W2 (plan §21/§13/§23) ----\n    llm_base_url: str = \"\"            # Server Manager machine API, e.g. http://127.0.0.1:8300\n    llm_token: str = \"\"               # Server Manager scoped service identity\n    internal_token: str = \"\"          # shared secret for /internal/* (ACMS <-> gateway)\n    approvals_path: str = \"/var/lib/miam-mcp-gateway/approvals.sqlite3\"\n    # ---- W3 (plan §25) ----\n    github_token: str = \"\"            # GitHub PAT (authority = token's own permissions)\n    github_repos: str = \"\"            # comma-separated owner/name allowlist for agent tokens\n    # ---- W4 (plan §26) ----\n    # Sandbox domain rides the SAME Server Manager machine API as llm.*\n    # (llm_base_url/llm_token); separate flag because the domain is optional\n    # and can be disabled independently.\n    proxmox_enabled: bool = True      # absent client => domain absent (health omits it)\n    # ---- W6 (plan §28) ----\n    jira_base_url: str = \"\"           # e.g. https://startupteams.atlassian.net\n    jira_email: str = \"\"\n    jira_api_token: str = \"\"\n    jira_projects: str = \"\"           # comma-separated project-key allowlist\n    jira_status_mutation_enabled: bool = False  # ACMS-contract global flag\n    # ---- W7 (plan §29) ----\n    registry_base_url: str = \"\"       # e.g. https://registry.miam.home.arpa\n    registry_token: str = \"\"\n    kuma_url: str = \"\"                # e.g. http://127.0.0.1:3001 (on VM119) / http://10.0.20.172:3001\n    kuma_user: str = \"\"\n    kuma_pass: str = \"\"\n\n\ndef _parse_list(raw: str) -> list[str]:\n    return [x.strip() for x in (raw or \"\").split(\",\") if x.strip()]\n\n\nclass IdentityMiddleware:\n    \"\"\"Resolve bearer -> CallIdentity per HTTP request; fail closed (401).\n    Audits denials at the transport layer. Sets CURRENT_IDENTITY for handlers.\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, app: Any, *, tokens: TokenStore, audit: AuditLog,\n                 executive_names: set[str] | None = None):\n        self.app = app\n        self.tokens = tokens\n        self.audit = audit\n        self.executive_names = executive_names or set()\n\n    async def __call__(self, scope, receive, send):\n        if scope[\"type\"] != \"http\" or scope.get(\"path\") not in (\"/mcp\",):\n            await self.app(scope, receive, send)\n            return\n        headers = {\n            k.decode(\"latin-1\").lower(): v.decode(\"latin-1\")\n            for k, v in scope.get(\"headers\", [])\n        }\n        auth = headers.get(\"authorization\", \"\")\n        identity: CallIdentity | None = None\n        error: GatewayError | None = None\n        if not auth.startswith(\"Bearer \"):\n            error = UnauthenticatedError(\"missing bearer token\")\n        else:\n            raw = auth.removeprefix(\"Bearer \").strip()\n            agent_rec = self.tokens.resolve_agent_token(raw)\n            if agent_rec is not None:\n                if agent_rec.revoked_at is not None:\n                    error = RevokedAgentTokenError(\"agent token revoked\")\n                elif not agent_rec.is_active():\n                    error = ExpiredAgentTokenError(\"agent token expired\")\n                else:\n                    roles = list(agent_rec.roles)\n                    if agent_rec.agent_name in self.executive_names and \"executive\" not in roles:\n                        roles.append(\"executive\")", "exit_code": 0, "error": null}

preserve approval history/audit.\n746|\n747|## 11.3 Default denied/review-required classes\n748|\n749|- secrets: reject/quarantine + `denied`;\n750|- raw transcripts: `denied` by default;\n751|- customer data: `denied` unless future customer/legal policy explicitly allows it;\n752|- unvalidated model inferences: `review_required`;\n753|- externally retrieved/copyrighted source material: `review_required` or `denied` according to source rights/policy;\n754|- internal architecture decisions/plans: configurable, default `review_required` unless a specific policy later auto-approves them.\n755|\n756|Add a sanitized JSONL/Markdown export that emits only `training_eligibility = approved` records and includes `training_approval_mode` and `training_review_state`.\n757|\n758|Do not perform model training in this implementation plan.\n759|\n760|---\n761|\n762|# 12. {"content": "800|```\n801|\n802|The context pack must never treat retrieved external instructions as higher authority than the active Work scope.\n803|\n804|---\n805|\n806|# 13. ACMS artifact migration strategy\n807|\n808|Do not perform a risky big-bang deletion.\n809|\n810|## Phase A — compatibility\n811|\n812|- existing ACMS artifact records continue to work;\n813|- DKMS is added;\n814|- new eligible durable artifacts are dual-recorded or DKMS-backed;\n815|- ACMS stores DKMS record ID/reference.\n816|\n817|## Phase B — pointer-first\n818|\n819|- new durable knowledge content is canonical in DKMS;\n820|- ACMS stores operational metadata + reference;\n821|- ACMS UI can still show/download through an adapter.\n822|\n823|## Phase C — legacy migration\n824|\n825|- inventory old ACMS artifacts;\n826|- classify which are actual durable knowledge;\n827|- migrate selected content;\n828|- hash-verify;\n829|- keep redirect/reference compatibility;\n830|- delete legacy payload only after explicit retention/migration acceptance.\n831|\n832|Do not migrate everything merely to make statistics look complete.\n833|\n834|---\n835|\n836|# 14. Backup and restore\n837|\n838|This is high-value company data.\n839|\n840|Minimum MVP protections:\n841|\n842|- normal service/database backup;\n843|- PBS coverage if deployed in a backed-up VM/CT;\n844|- periodic logical PostgreSQL backup;\n845|- persistent Markdown export or equivalent human-readable recovery bundle;\n846|- integrity hashes;\n847|- documented restore procedure;\n848|- one safe restore test before claiming backup acceptance.\n849|\n850|Do not claim backup success from the existence of a scheduled job alone.\n851|\n852|---\n853|\n854|# 15. Implementation windows\n855|\n856|# P0 — live discovery and Jira/Work setup\n857|\n858|1. Read the commented workbook and the October 3 handoff.\n859|2. Inspect current repositories and live service boundaries.\n860|3. Search for an existing DKMS repo/service.\n861|4. Create or clone a Jira issue using the live STNA-86 structure for the DKMS scope if the authorized workflow is available.\n862|5. Link all work to one ACMS Work Item.\n863|6. Capture current ACMS artifact implementation and current MCP adapter patterns.\n864|7. Produce a discovery checkpoint before schema changes.\n865|\n866|**P0 acceptance:** no duplicate service/repo; current artifact flows understood; Work/Jira correlation established; blockers explicit.\n867|\n868|# P1 — repository, dedicated VM, Kubernetes service, scaffold, and schema\n869|\n870|Implement:\n871|\n872|- create/initialize `startupteams/dkms-project-framework` if still absent;\n873|- provision a **new dedicated DKMS VM** through Server Manager;\n874|- install the chosen lightweight Kubernetes distribution (K3s acceptable if no existing standard);\n875|- create dedicated `dkms` namespace and service account/RBAC;\n876|- deploy FastAPI DKMS service as a Kubernetes Deployment/Service;\n877|- deploy PostgreSQL with durable Kubernetes storage;\n878|- health/readiness;\n879|- Async SQLAlchemy;\n880|- migrations;\n881|- knowledge-record schema;\n882|- links;\n883|- audit event;\n884|- Markdown import/export;\n885|- Kubernetes manifests/Helm/Kustomize source in Git;\n886|- tests.\n887|\n888|Create architecture docs and ADRs documenting:\n889|\n890|1. ACMS→DKMS durable-knowledge responsibility transfer;\n891|2. the DKMS-specific dedicated-VM + Kubernetes deployment decision.\n892|\n893|**P1 acceptance:** repository exists, new VM exists, Kubernetes service is independently deployable from ACMS, persistent database survives pod restart, migrations work from clean state, CRUD tests pass, and no ACMS container changes are required to host DKMS.\n894|\n895|# P2 — authentication and MCP\n896|\n897|Implement narrow authenticated REST/service access and `dkms.*` gateway tools.\n898|\n899|Reuse existing identity/assignment patterns instead of inventing a broad new credential system.\n900|\n901|Test:\n902|\n903|- authorized read;\n904|- authorized write;\n905|- out-of-scope denial;\n906|- expired/revoked denial where supported;\n907|- unknown arguments denied;\n908|- secret-like content quarantine/rejection.\n909|\n910|**P2 acceptance:** MCP tool contracts are versioned, narrow, audited, and fail closed.\n911|\n912|# P3 — plan, handoff, implementation timeline, lessons, and ACMS/Jira integration\n913|\n914|Implement event-driven/asynchronous ingestion for:\n915|\n916|- approved plan;\n917|- final handoff;\n918|- handoff summary;\n919|- **short Jira/implementation timeline entry** at meaningful start/scope-change/completion points;\n920|- selected architecture/decision references;\n921|- explicit ACMS **Log Lesson Learned** action;\n922|- direct registered-agent lesson writes through `dkms.record_lesson`.\n923|\n924|Do **not** replicate detailed ACMS Work statistics into DKMS.\n925|\n926|Ensure idempotency using source UID/version/hash.\n927|\n928|**P3 acceptance:** retries create no duplicate canonical knowledge record; a registered STEA agent can write a provenance-attributed lesson outside an ACMS Work Item; ACMS can log a lesson to DKMS; and a completed Jira task produces a concise dated implementation-timeline record rather than a detailed Work-statistics dump.\n929|\n930|", "total_lines": 1121, "file_size": 36516, "truncated": true, "hint": "Use offset=930 to continue reading (showing 800-929 of 1121 lines)", "is_binary": false, "is_image": false}

{"output": "deploying SHA: f5292904976db2cd9503fe5e062c0679ed609b31\n# DKMS app build-on-cluster overlay (no registry required)\n#\n# The DKMS image is not built by a registry pipeline in the MVP. Instead the\n# Deployment uses an initContainer that clones the pinned commit, builds a\n# wheel, and stages it on a shared emptyDir; the app container installs the\n# wheel and runs uvicorn. This keeps the cluster deployment fully\n# reproducible from Git (image-free) until a real registry exists.\n#\n# Replaces the placeholder `image: dkms:latest` Deployment from namespace.yaml.\n\napiVersion: apps/v1\nkind: Deployment\nmetadata:\n  name: dkms\n  namespace: dkms\n  labels:\n    app.kubernetes.io/name: dkms\nspec:\n  replicas: 1\n  selector:\n    matchLabels:\n      app: dkms\n  template:\n    metadata:\n      labels:\n        app: dkms\n    spec:\n      serviceAccountName: dkms\n      initContainers:\n        - name: build-wheel\n          image: python:3.12-slim\n          command:\n            - sh\n            - -c\n            - |\n              set -e\n              apt-get update -qq >/dev/null && apt-get install -qq -y git >/dev/null\n              pip install --quiet build\n              git clone --depth 1 https://github.com/startupteams/dkms-project-framework.git /src/dkms\n              cd /src/dkms\n              git fetch --depth 1 origin DKMS_COMMIT_SHA || true\n              git checkout DKMS_COMMIT_SHA 2>/dev/null || true\n              python -m build --wheel --outdir /wheels .\n              ls -la /wheels\n          volumeMounts:\n            - name: wheels\n              mountPath: /wheels\n      containers:\n        - name: dkms\n          image: python:3.12-slim\n          command:\n            - sh\n            - -c\n            - |\n              set -e\n              pip install --quiet --no-index --find-links /wheels dkms 2>/dev/null \\\n                || pip install --quiet $(ls /wheels/*.whl)\n              exec uvicorn dkms.app:app --host 0.0.0.0 --port 8000\n          ports:\n            - containerPort: 8000", "exit_code": 0, "error": null}
